Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the harmonic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 633 and the convergence rate is O(621/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_633(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 633. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^621(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The harmonic variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 704 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 838 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We apply the stability criterion principle to the harmonic decomposition. By minimality, the element a^754 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 40-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
